# Skills · G · part 31

897 entries, genomic-coordinates to geo. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **genomic-coordinates**: Converts genomic intervals between coordinate conventions, normalises and compares variant representations, and detects assembly or contig-naming mismatches before they corrupt an analysis. Used whenever coordinates cross a format, tool, or assembly boundary - converting between BED, GFF/GTF, VCF, SAM/BAM, WIG, PSL, genePred, Picard interval_list, or region strings; reconciling 0-based half-open with 1-based inclusive; left-aligning or trimming indels; checking whether two variant records descri ([k-dense-ai/scientific-agent-skills](https://github.com/k-dense-ai/scientific-agent-skills) · 1.2k installs)
- **genomic-correlation-decay-visualization**: Use when integrating epigenomic p-values (e.g., DNA methylation) whose assay sites are spatially non-independent, and you need to quantify or visualize how correlation between adjacent CpG p-values decays with genomic distance before combining p-values. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/genomic-correlation-decay-visualization))
- **genomic-count-matrix-construction**: Use when raw ChIP-Seq BAM alignments plus an experiment design file must be converted into a tiled, normalized count matrix too large for memory, as preprocessing before GAM model fitting. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/genomic-count-matrix-construction))
- **genomic-coverage-track-generation**: Use when paired-end MNase-seq or ATAC-seq BAM alignments must be converted into fragment-length-classified, midpoint-aggregated bedGraph count tracks to serve as HMM observables for chromatin occupancy profiling. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/genomic-coverage-track-generation))
- **genomic-data-commons**: Query, search, and download cancer genomics data from the NCI Genomic Data Commons (GDC) REST API. Use when the user needs to find cases or files in TCGA, TARGET, CPTAC, FM, BEATAML, HCMI, MMRF, CGCI, or other GDC programs; pull clinical, biospecimen, copy-number, or somatic-mutation metadata; download open-access files or generate Data Transfer Tool manifests; perform BAM slicing by gene or region; retrieve gene expression matrices (FPKM-UQ), top-mutated-genes/SSMs analyses, survival data, or scRNA-Seq expression; manage saved cohorts; or run ad-hoc GraphQL queries against the GDC schema. ([jataware/beaker-biomedical](https://github.com/jataware/beaker-biomedical/tree/HEAD/src/beaker_biomedical/beaker_biomedical_context/skills/genomics-data-commons))
- **genomic-data-privacy-governance**: Use when a task involves planning privacy controls for human genomic or multi-omics data used in research and agent workflows to identify the research or operational question, target population or system, relevant version, sensitive data, and approval boundary before acting. Use current primary sources, produce a traceable artifact, and verify it against explicit criteria. Trigger for planning, analysis, review, or troubleshooting in this focused domain; do not execute external writes or clinical actions without authorization. ([Manoj-11-Dahal/try-Skills](https://github.com/Manoj-11-Dahal/try-Skills/tree/HEAD/skills/science-research/life-sciences/genomic-data-privacy-governance) · ★3)
- **genomic-data-train-valid-test-splitting**: Use when raw task data downloaded for a DNALONGBENCH task must be partitioned and serialized into train*/valid*/test*.tfr files so downstream loaders and model training consume consistent splits. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/genomic-data-train-valid-test-splitting))
- **genomic-database-identifier-mapping**: Use when your gene expression matrix or pathway collection uses identifier formats incompatible with your enrichment analysis tool (e.g., gene symbols vs. Entrez IDs), or you need to reconcile gene sets from multiple sources (Reactome, KEGG) that employ different naming conventions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v1/skills/genomic-database-identifier-mapping))
- **genomic-distance-background-modeling**: Use when distance-normalized Hi-C or pcHi-C contact tables (covNorm normDistance output) need per-contact significance scores (p-value and FDR) so that reproducible significant long-range interactions can be detected. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/genomic-distance-background-modeling))
- **genomic-distance-binning-analysis**: Use when coverage-normalized Hi-C or pcHi-C fragment-pair tables (Rij residuals with cov_frag1, cov_frag2, freq, dist columns) need distance-dependent background removal before calling significant long-range interactions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/genomic-distance-binning-analysis))
- **genomic-distance-normalization-of-contact-probabilities**: Use when calling chromatin loop candidates from single-cell Hi-C contact matrices at 10-kb bin resolution and you need to control for the monotonic distance decay of contact probability before declaring a bin pair significant. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/genomic-distance-normalization-of-contact-probabilities))
- **genomic-distance-threshold-filtering**: Use when preparing Hi-C fragment files for haplotype assembly with SpecHap and you need to drop read pairs spanning more than 40 Mbp to avoid linkages with elevated trans interaction error rates. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/genomic-distance-threshold-filtering))
- **genomic-domain-boundary-refinement**: Use when calling broad ChIP-seq enrichment domains (e.g., H3K27me3/H3K9me3, kb-to-Mb) and you must convert multi-scale coarse-grained '1' blocks into precisely bounded candidate domains prior to Poisson/FDR significance assessment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/genomic-domain-boundary-refinement))
- **genomic-enhancer-prediction**: Use when the goal is to classify genomic regions as enhancers from matched ChIP-seq (H3K4me1, H3K4me3, H3K27ac) and DNase-seq datasets, e.g. reproducing enhancer catalogs in human cell lines like HCT116 or HeLa. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/genomic-enhancer-prediction))
- **genomic-epidemiology**: Pathogen genomics for epidemiology — phylogenetics of outbreaks, transmission inference, and variant surveillance. ([aicodedecode/awesome-muse-skills](https://github.com/aicodedecode/awesome-muse-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/genomic-epidemiology) · ★68 · 3 copies)
- **genomic-epidemiology-reporting**: Use when in-silico virulence/typing gene calls from sequencing reads must be validated against PCR or another phenotypic reference across a clinical isolate cohort, with per-isolate concordance reported. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/genomic-epidemiology-reporting))
- **genomic-feature-annotation**: This skill is used to perform genomic feature annotation and visualization for any file containing genomic region information using Homer (Hypergeometric Optimization of Motif EnRichment). It annotates regions such as promoters, exons, introns, intergenic regions, and TSS proximity, and generates visual summaries of feature distributions. ChIPseeker mode is also supported according to requirements. ([diegosouzapw/awesome-omni-skill](https://github.com/diegosouzapw/awesome-omni-skill/tree/HEAD/skills/product/genomic-feature-annotation-majiayu000) · ★67 · 5 copies)
- **genomic-feature-annotation-overlap**: Use when after you have identified a set of differentially methylated bases or regions (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v1/skills/genomic-feature-annotation-overlap))
- **genomic-feature-collinearity-diagnostics**: Use when a multi-score genomic feature table (e.g., variant scores from multiple predictors plus epigenomic annotations) must be checked for pairwise redundancy/collinearity before feeding features into an integrative model such as ncDeepBrain. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/genomic-feature-collinearity-diagnostics))
- **genomic-feature-correlation-analysis**: Use when you have per-region expression estimates (e.g., uTAR pseudo-bulk coverage) plus a PCA loading matrix from single-cell expression data and need to ask whether the most highly expressed features are also the ones that define cell types. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/genomic-feature-correlation-analysis))
- **genomic-feature-engineering-from-chromatin-signals**: Use when genomic elements (candidate enhancers vs promoters) must be converted into numeric features from ChIP-seq/DNase-seq bedGraph signals to train or apply an enhancer-prediction classifier. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/genomic-feature-engineering-from-chromatin-signals))
- **genomic-feature-importance-ranking**: Use when you have labeled genomic interval sets (e.g. HOT vs COLD regions) with coordinates and reference genome sequences, and need to rank sequence or genomic features by predictive importance. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/genomic-feature-importance-ranking))
- **genomic-feature-interval-derivation**: Use when you have a UCSC refGene.txt transcript annotation file plus chromInfo.txt for a genome assembly and need BED-like coordinate sets per feature class to feed feature-level analyses (e.g. scorePhastCons conservation scores). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/genomic-feature-interval-derivation))
- **genomic-feature-missingness-statistics**: Use when preparing variant-prioritization training matrices from ANNOVAR-annotated score columns (DANN, CADD, EIGEN, GWAVA, FATHMM) and you need to quantify per-score missingness before/after bPCA imputation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/genomic-feature-missingness-statistics))
- **genomic-feature-size-distribution-statistics**: Use when a catalog of genomic features (e.g. eccDNAs from WGS analysis plus literature curation) must be reduced to headline distribution statistics — total/provenance counts, mean/median feature size, per-feature segment counts, and copy numbers — for reproducibility checks. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/genomic-feature-size-distribution-statistics))
- **genomic-file-toolkit**: 当需要读写或解析 SAM/BAM/CRAM 比对、VCF/BCF 变异、FASTA/FASTQ 序列并提取区间、计算覆盖度时使用；用 pysam（htslib）做区域抓取、pileup 深度统计、变异过滤与序列提取，产出过滤后文件或统计结果；不适用于纯文本表格清洗、对齐/变异检测算法本身的实现、非基因组数据。触发词：pysam、bam、vcf、fastq、cram、bcf、fasta、samtools、bcftools、tabix、pileup、coverage、覆盖度、测序比对、变异、基因组区间、reference sequence、NGS、生物信息 ([findscripter/everything-skills](https://github.com/findscripter/everything-skills/tree/HEAD/09-verticals/genomic-file-toolkit) · ★11 · 2 copies)
- **genomic-flanking-sequence-extraction**: Use when variants (e.g., a VCF annotated by ANNOVAR ensGene) need underlying genomic sequence — exon plus flanks — retrieved from hg19 to build feature tables for splice-impact prediction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/genomic-flanking-sequence-extraction))
- **genomic-flanking-sequence-retrieval**: Use when you have coordinate-annotated modification sites (e.g., BED-format Ψ sites from RMBase) and need fixed-width sequence fragments centered on each site from a matching reference genome for downstream ML feature encoding. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/genomic-flanking-sequence-retrieval))
- **genomic-graph-representation-construction**: Use when input is a tab-separated N×N intra-chromosomal Hi-C contact matrix (e.g. dumped from .hic data via juicer_tools) and the goal is to represent genomic bins as graph nodes for GAE-based feature learning or clustering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/genomic-graph-representation-construction))
- **genomic-indel-feature-preparation**: Use when building supervised predictors for non-coding small indels (sindels) that need both generic functional annotations and tissue-specific epigenomic sequence-derived features, e.g. replicating the TIVAN-indel feature-set ablation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/genomic-indel-feature-preparation))
- **genomic-inquiry**: Default entry for natural-language DNA questions. The host agent resolves intent, reads focused skills, calls narrow evidence tools, and adapts after inspecting tool output. ([exon-research/genomi](https://github.com/exon-research/genomi/tree/HEAD/skills/genomic-inquiry) · ★493 · 2 copies)

Six tasks over a REST API and a hosted MCP server (keyless public demo): promoter regions, splice donor/acceptor sites, enhancer activity, chromatin state, sequence-to-expression (log TPM), and de-novo gene annotation, plus a composite find-genes-then-predict-expression workflow. - **genomic-interaction-distance-filtering**: Use when comparing per-cell chromatin interaction counts across single-cell Hi-C protocols (sci-Hi-C, sci-L3-Hi-C, s3-GCC, droplet Hi-C) where raw valid-pair counts are inflated by linear-amplification (IVT) duplicates and short-distance interactions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/genomic-interaction-distance-filtering))
- **genomic-interaction-table-processing**: Use when input is a tab-delimited Hi-C or capture Hi-C fragment-pair table (frag1, frag2, cov_frag1, cov_frag2, freq, dist) and the goal is to remove coverage-driven ligation bias from raw interaction frequencies before distance normalization or contact calling. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/genomic-interaction-table-processing))
- **genomic-interval-aggregation**: Use when a broad-domain ChIP-seq analysis needs mapped BED reads binned into fixed windows (e.g., 200 bp) with 5'-end shifting (e.g., 75 bp for 150 bp fragments) before multi-scale enrichment detection. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/genomic-interval-aggregation))
- **genomic-interval-anchoring-quantification**: Use when you have a set of called TAD boundaries (start bin, start location, end bin, end location) plus genomic peak/interval files (narrowPeak BED, enhancer/promoter BED) and need anchoring ratios per regulatory factor. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/genomic-interval-anchoring-quantification))
- **genomic-interval-bed-processing**: Use when a simulation or analysis must be restricted to a specific chromosome plus exome capture regions, and reference FASTA, .dict/.fai indices, and known-variant VCFs (gnomAD, Mills, 1000G, dbSNP) need region-specific subsets. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/genomic-interval-bed-processing))
- **genomic-interval-binning-and-visualization**: Reference for converting significant ChIA-PET loop calls (bedpe) into a 10 kb-binned contact matrix and visually comparing it against Hi-C data at loop anchors. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/genomic-interval-binning-and-visualization))
- **genomic-interval-chain-liftover**: Use when lifting non-coding regulatory regions (peaks, GWAS SNP loci) between species with a UCSC chain file and candidates must be ranked by functional (epigenomic/motif) similarity rather than position alone. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/genomic-interval-chain-liftover))
- **genomic-interval-consolidation**: Use when you have an exogenous-m6A-labeled nanopore genomic DNA modBAM and need to convert modkit pileup signals into consolidated, high-confidence open-chromatin intervals and per-genome BedGraph tracks. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/genomic-interval-consolidation))
- **genomic-interval-coordinate-handling**: Use when you must convert ENCODE/modENCODE narrowPeak ChIP-seq peak files into per-genome interval tracks (e.g. HOT regions) by tallying peak-summit coordinates over sliding genomic windows. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/genomic-interval-coordinate-handling))
- **genomic-interval-coverage-calculation**: Use when you need to quantify how completely called broad ChIP-seq domains (e.g., ENCODE broadPeak-format H3K27me3 domains) cover annotated gene bodies of repressed genes across multiple methods or samples. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/genomic-interval-coverage-calculation))
- **genomic-interval-distance-matching**: Use when processed ChIP-seq peak interval files (e.g. MACS2 LuxR and H-NS peaks from GEO GSE136191/GSE136050) must be intersected or distance-matched, such as counting co-occupied LuxR/H-NS loci per strain or flagging H-NS doublets within 1 kb of a LuxR peak. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/genomic-interval-distance-matching))
- **genomic-interval-extension-and-merging**: Use when raw ChIP-seq/DNase narrow-peak BED files (chrom, start, end) must be converted into fixed-width genomic training windows with a fixed CNN input size and no redundant overlap before training. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/genomic-interval-extension-and-merging))
- **genomic-interval-extraction-around-editing-sites**: Use when you have REDItools outTables of candidate A-to-G substitutions and need fixed-length 101-nt centered sequence contexts (with per-position nucleotide frequencies) to feed a deep-learning editing classifier such as REDInet. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/genomic-interval-extraction-around-editing-sites))
- **genomic-interval-extraction-from-gene-annotation**: Use when you need to obtain concrete genomic coordinates from a gene annotation table (UCSC knownGenes track) to define fixed-length regions — e.g. 2 kb windows spanning TSS and first splicing site — on which ChIP-Seq enrichment profiles will be extracted. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/genomic-interval-extraction-from-gene-annotation))
- **genomic-interval-granges-manipulation**: Use when coordinate-based query regions (e.g., fine-mapped BMD GWAS SNPs at PIP ≥0.001, or epigenome peak sets) must be loaded as genomic intervals in R and lifted over between hg38 and mm10 via a UCSC chain file. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/genomic-interval-granges-manipulation))
- **genomic-interval-intersection**: Use when a set of predicted regulatory element coordinates (e.g. scATAC-seq candidate CREs) needs to be intersected with a reference table of experimentally validated enhancers to annotate functional status per region. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/genomic-interval-intersection))
- **genomic-interval-intersection**: Use when you need to corroborate 5′ ends (TSSs) of long-read-derived isoforms against independent 5′-end-enriched short-read data such as CAGE-seq, computing a per-isoform coverage-supported validation fraction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/genomic-interval-intersection))
- **genomic-interval-intersection**: Use when predicted genomic features (e.g., aptardi polyA-site bins in BED format) must be validated against reference annotation intervals (PolyASite 2.0, PolyA_DB 3) to derive TP/FP classifications and precision/recall metrics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/genomic-interval-intersection))
- **genomic-interval-intersection-computation**: Use when scoring ChIP-seq peak calls against ENCODE union DHS regions to compute per-sample overlap ratios, or when grouping overlap ratios by histone mark / factor category to build a quality atlas distribution. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/genomic-interval-intersection-computation))
- **genomic-interval-intersection-with-bedtools**: Use when ChIA-PIPE has produced peak-supported loops (PET count &gt;= 3) with unique loop IDs from RNAPII ChIA-PET data and you must annotate each loop as E-P, P-P, or neither using enhancer/promoter BED annotations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/genomic-interval-intersection-with-bedtools))
- **genomic-interval-length-filtering**: Use when you have per-bin labels (e.g., HDBSCAN clusters on Hi-C genomic bins at 50/25/5 Kb resolution) and need contiguous same-label regions filtered by a genomic length threshold to define TAD boundaries. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/genomic-interval-length-filtering))
- **genomic-interval-merging**: Use when peak- or loop-supported genomic intervals must be collapsed into contiguous domains, e.g., deriving CCDs from ChIA-PIPE CTCF loop BED output where overlapping/adjacent loops define continuous coverage. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/genomic-interval-merging))
- **genomic-interval-merging**: Use when per-read, window-level low-entropy or feature-bearing coordinates must be coalesced into non-overlapping genomic intervals (e.g., BED regions) prior to tandem repeat discovery or coverage-filtered profiling. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/genomic-interval-merging))
- **genomic-interval-merging**: Use when strand-specific predicted intervals (e.g., groHMM TAR predictions on BED files) must be consolidated into non-overlapping features within a distance threshold such as 500 bp before coverage filtering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/genomic-interval-merging))
- **genomic-interval-nearest-matching**: Use when predicted genomic intervals (e.g. IPA sites from InPACT, APAIQ, IPAFinder) must be validated against reference polyA site sets (A-seq, 3P-seq, polyA-seq, Iso-seq) by nearest-feature distance rather than exact overlap. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/genomic-interval-nearest-matching))
- **genomic-interval-operations**: Use when genomic intervals (e.g., ATAC-seq or ChIP-seq peak BED regions) in one genome assembly (hg38, mm10, rn6) must be lifted over, partitioned by mapping multiplicity, or compared against intervals in another species using UCSC chain files and GRanges. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/genomic-interval-operations))
- **genomic-interval-overlap-analysis**: Use when comparing peak/enriched-window calls from multiple CLIP-seq analysis methods run on the same matched IP-and-input eCLIP replicates, and you need per-RBP counts of shared vs. unique binding sites via interval intersection. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/genomic-interval-overlap-analysis--7638b350ae376bc5))
- **genomic-interval-overlap-analysis**: Use when you have strand-annotated BED files of motifs (e.g. PolyASite signals, RepeatMasker Alu elements, TFBSs) and regions (e.g. GENCODE protein-coding genes) and want to test whether motif orientation is biased relative to regions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/genomic-interval-overlap-analysis--f61222d888c179f6))
- **genomic-interval-overlap-analysis**: Use when you have two or more structural-variant call sets in reference coordinates and need to isolate tool-specific calls (e.g., inversions found by invMap but missed by competing mappers). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/genomic-interval-overlap-analysis--fd472c8e64fd53a7))
- **genomic-interval-overlap-analysis**: Use when you need to quantify how many nanopore (or Illumina) reads fall inside annotated genomic intervals (ChIP-seq peaks, CpG islands) or to compare read-based metrics across feature classes and technologies. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/genomic-interval-overlap-analysis--7ccbc4a8927288e5))
- **genomic-interval-overlap-analysis**: Use when validating predicted genomic regions (e.g., dsRID dsRNA BED files) against an experimentally derived interval set (e.g., J2 fCLIP-seq peaks) by intersecting coordinates and quantifying overlap fractions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/genomic-interval-overlap-analysis--3be4d2af2162aefb))
- **genomic-interval-overlap-analysis**: Use when you already have a scHiCDiff DCI results data frame (bin pairs with pvalue.adj.FDR) and need downstream functional annotation: epigenetic mark association at DCI bins and GO enrichment of DCI-linked genes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/genomic-interval-overlap-analysis--b74fc9e7e3b09e61))
- **genomic-interval-overlap-annotation**: Use when per-region feature values (e.g. enhancer/promoter probabilities from bin-level tracks) must be transferred onto annotated genomic intervals (cCRE-ELS, TSSs) as input features for downstream ET-pair classification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/genomic-interval-overlap-annotation))
- **genomic-interval-overlap-classification**: Use when phasiRNA/PHAS loci predicted against both a transcriptome and a genome reference must be de-duplicated into reference-unique sets and each unique locus classified by exon/intron structure from a gff3 annotation (e.g. mirroring Supplementary Fig. S3). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/genomic-interval-overlap-classification))
- **genomic-interval-overlap-comparison**: Use when you have two BED-format peak lists for different histone marks or features and need to partition their union into overlapping vs. non-overlapping classes (e.g. H3K4me3+/H3K27me3– bivalent analysis). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/genomic-interval-overlap-comparison))
- **genomic-interval-overlap-counting**: Use when comparing null distributions of random overlap counts between region sets over different genomic or transcriptome backgrounds, e.g., quantifying how many overlaps arise by chance over DNA vs mRNA permutation spaces. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/genomic-interval-overlap-counting))
- **genomic-interval-overlap-filtering**: Use when you have per-replicate significant interaction/loop calls (e.g., FitHiC at FDR &lt; 0.05, HiCCUPS or Mustache loops) and a list of significant differential compartment bins (e.g., RefineY_Rconf90_FDR10), and need only the interactions anchored in those bins. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/genomic-interval-overlap-filtering))
- **genomic-interval-overlap-filtering**: Use when a variant database such as ClinVar must be subset to exome/target capture regions on a specific chromosome and its output harmonised to the reference genome format before simulation or calling. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/genomic-interval-overlap-filtering))
- **genomic-interval-overlap-matching**: Use when comparing loop-calling results (bedpe anchor coordinates) against a curated reference interaction set and you need TP/FP/FN counts, precision, recall, and F1 per method and per subsampling level. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/genomic-interval-overlap-matching))
- **genomic-interval-overlap-matrix**: Use when multiple m6A detection tools have returned genomic BED call sets on the same dRNA-seq dataset and you need to quantify how concordant their calls are, independent of any ground-truth reference. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/genomic-interval-overlap-matrix))
- **genomic-interval-overlap-merging**: Use when you have sorted, coordinate-anchored genomic intervals (e.g. REM BED records with chr, start, end) and need to collapse overlaps/adjacencies into clusters and quantify their span and multiplicity. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/genomic-interval-overlap-merging))
- **genomic-interval-overlap-quantification**: Use when two or more sets of genomic intervals (e.g. DMRs from different callers, i-DMRs vs reference regions) must be intersected to compute overlap fractions, or when CpG-level methylation records need region names from a BED annotation file before running DXM. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/genomic-interval-overlap-quantification--e6ba55f22be7112c))
- **genomic-interval-overlap-quantification**: Use when you have BHi-Cect cluster/loci assignments and coordinate-based epigenomic annotation tracks, and need per-enclave overlap counts and enrichment values to test how activity varies with nestedness along the BPT. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/genomic-interval-overlap-quantification--f8c5c4f8231f0dd1))
- **genomic-interval-pairing-and-slop-extension**: Use when a MAPIT-seq analysis needs to convert filtered editing sites into high-confidence RBP-binding regions by requiring adjacent C-to-U and A-to-G edit clusters reproducible across replicates. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/genomic-interval-pairing-and-slop-extension))
- **genomic-interval-processing**: Use when the task requires comparing, merging, or signal-quantifying genomic intervals, e.g. calling CC peaks from qbed insertions, annotating peaks, or computing ChIP-seq signal under called peaks. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/genomic-interval-processing))
- **genomic-interval-promoter-assignment**: Use when you have a set of ChIP-seq peak intervals (e.g., MACs2 output for two regulators) plus RNA-seq differential expression calls, and you need to assign peaks to promoters/ORFs and count co-regulated, co-occupied loci. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/genomic-interval-promoter-assignment))
- **genomic-interval-sequence-extraction**: Use when converting tabix-indexed REDItools outTables into per-site 8×101 feature matrices for TCN-based editing classification, e.g. filtering candidates (cov≥50, ≥3 Gs, A-to-G freq≥0.01) and pulling flanking sequence from a faidx-indexed genome. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/genomic-interval-sequence-extraction))
- **genomic-landmark-position-histogramming**: Use when evaluating whether a DTW-based clustering/alignment of epigenomic peak regions has sharpened the positions of annotated genomic landmarks (TSS, FSS) within each cluster. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/genomic-landmark-position-histogramming))
- **genomic-locus-subsetting-of-modification-calls**: Use when you have per-replicate modkit modification site tables (e.g. filtered .bed.gz from Dogme) and need to count or intersect modification sites within one gene or region, such as testing reproducibility at the Pkm locus. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/genomic-locus-subsetting-of-modification-calls))
- **genomic-locus-to-cluster-mapping**: Use when a BHi-Cect tree output (nested cluster list / named loci lists) must be joined with gene coordinates and per-locus ChIP-seq signal (e.g. RNAP2 in IMR90, H3K4me3 in K562) to produce per-enclave gene rankings for enrichment analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/genomic-locus-to-cluster-mapping))
- **genomic-loop-call-interpretation**: Use when you have a pre-generated .hic contact map file and need to identify and annotate chromatin loops or topologically associating domains (TADs) at high resolution. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v1/skills/genomic-loop-call-interpretation))
- **genomic-mappability-and-blacklist-filtering**: Use when running SnapHiC (or similar loop-calling pipelines) on scHi-C/sn-m3C-seq contact files and you must restrict loop candidate calling to bins with adequate mappability, excluding ENCODE blacklist overlaps. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/genomic-mappability-and-blacklist-filtering))
- **genomic-matrix-missing-value-filtering**: Use when a raw numeric expression/methylation matrix destined for cola consensus partitioning contains missing values, extreme outliers, or rows with very small variance that would distort row-wise scaling and feature selection. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/genomic-matrix-missing-value-filtering))
- **genomic-metabolomic-link-ranking**: Use when you have paired genomic (BGCs clustered into GCFs via BiG-SCAPE) and metabolomic data (MS2 spectra grouped into MFs), with strain/sample co-occurrence patterns and predicted BGC–spectrum IOKR scores, and you need to prioritise which GCF–MF pairs are most likely to represent true natural. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/genomic-metabolomic-link-ranking) · 2 copies)
- **genomic-negative-region-sampling**: Use when building a positive/negative benchmark for genomic prediction tools (e.g. 3plex TPX scoring): you have experimentally derived peak sets (IDR, Overlap q&lt;0. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/genomic-negative-region-sampling))
- **genomic-overlap-merging**: Use when a viral scaffold FASTA may contain redundant/overlapping sequences from fragmented genome assemblies and a dereplicated representative set is required before binning. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/genomic-overlap-merging))
- **genomic-overlap-strand-assignment**: Use when a BED feature set lacks strand annotation but an overlapping strand-assigned feature set exists (e.g., orienting hg38 CTCF ChIP-seq peaks using MA0139.1 CTCF motif occurrences) prior to strand asymmetry analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/genomic-overlap-strand-assignment))
- **genomic-position-deduplication**: Use when you hold per-read methylation call tables (read_id, chromosome, pos_in_strand, strand, methylation_rate) or BS-seq calls and need unique-site CpG coverage counts per dataset rather than per-read statistics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/genomic-position-deduplication))
- **genomic-position-file-parsing**: Use when constructing Delila-PY inputs: you have ChIP-seq/ChIP-chip peak centers or transcription start sites and need to encode each location with chromosome, strand, and base position, then define -l/-r boundaries for motif extraction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/genomic-position-file-parsing))
- **genomic-prediction**: Use when building, validating, or reporting genomic prediction or polygenic scores in crops, livestock, or humans. Not for general design (study-design) or association testing (association-studies). ([oumarkme/research_agents](https://github.com/oumarkme/research_agents/tree/HEAD/plugins/bioinfo-lab/skills/genomic-prediction))
- **genomic-prediction**: Execution blueprints and code templates for fitting genomic prediction models, including GBLUP, GFBLUP, MultiBLUP, Bayesian methods, and Random Forest using peer-reviewed software. Strictly incorporates robust self-auditing, high-clarity code commenting, big data performance safeguards, and strict matrix alignment rules. ([scicrow/genomic-prediction-skill](https://github.com/scicrow/genomic-prediction-skill/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md))
- **genomic-proximity-gene-mapping**: Use when you have a set of sentinel variants (e.g. mQTLs or cis-pQTLs with GRCh37 coordinates) and need to prioritize or validate causal gene assignments based on genomic proximity, LD-overlap, or cis-eQTL targeting. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/genomic-proximity-gene-mapping))
- **genomic-range-block-bootstrap**: Use when testing enrichment or correlation of genomic ranges (e.g. caQTL peaks vs GWAS SNPs, DEG vs differential accessibility peaks, CRE-gene correlations across pseudo-bulk cell types) against a null and conventional shuffling is at risk of an overly narrow null distribution. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/genomic-range-block-bootstrap))
- **genomic-range-enrichment-analysis**: Use when testing enrichment of overlaps between genomic range sets (e.g. caQTL SNPs vs chromatin accessibility peaks, or DEGs vs DA peaks) and when permutation-based nulls seem too narrow or thresholds need optimization. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/genomic-range-enrichment-analysis))
- **genomic-range-overlap-annotation**: Use when annotating sentinel (and LD-proxied) variants with positional candidate genes from variant-level data — nearest-gene extraction, LD-range overlap, and biotype filtering around molecular QTL loci. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/genomic-range-overlap-annotation))
- **genomic-range-overlap-statistics**: Use when testing whether an observed overlap between two genomic range sets (e.g. caQTL/SNP sets and peaks, or CREs and genes) is significant relative to a null that accounts for genomic structure, using GRanges objects in R/Bioconductor. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/genomic-range-overlap-statistics))
- **genomic-range-simulation-under-null**: Use when testing overlap/enrichment between two genomic range sets (e.g. caQTLs vs GWAS SNPs, CREs vs genes) and a properly calibrated null is needed, especially when local genomic correlation structure must be preserved. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/genomic-range-simulation-under-null))
- **genomic-read-to-gene-overlap-assignment**: Use when Nanopore dRNA-seq read alignments (BAM) must be assigned to genes and split into mature vs premature classes using GTF intron overlap, e.g. before quantifying RNA pools in subcellular fractions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/genomic-read-to-gene-overlap-assignment))
- **genomic-reference-library-processing**: Use when building a Kraken-compatible taxonomy database from NCBI RefSeq complete microbial genomes so that exact k-mer lookups resolve to specific taxon IDs for metagenomic classification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/genomic-reference-library-processing))
- **genomic-region-annotation-integration**: Use when after bias-correcting ATAC-seq cutsite signal (via ATACorrect) when you have a bias-corrected bigWig file and need to compute per-position footprint scores within defined accessible regions (peaks, called footprints, or regulatory regions) to detect and quantify transcription factor. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v1/skills/genomic-region-annotation-integration))
- **genomic-region-based-job-distribution**: Use when running scywalker's align-and-reconstruct stages on whole genomes where splitting work over chromosomes or sub-chromosomal regions is needed to parallelise and bound memory. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/genomic-region-based-job-distribution))
- **genomic-region-bed-interval-handling**: Use when you need read-level methylation linkage (phase) information within defined genomic intervals from MethylC-seq/Bismark alignments, e.g. to test whether methylation differences occur in a nonrandom pattern. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/genomic-region-bed-interval-handling))
- **genomic-region-blacklist-filtering**: Reference for removing non-informative or artifact-prone regions (sex chromosomes, chrM, ENCODE blacklist) from bin-sample methylation quantification matrices before downstream group/batch comparisons. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/genomic-region-blacklist-filtering))
- **genomic-region-coordinate-aggregation**: Use when individual-CpG methylation count tables (e.g. Bismark-derived .cov files with methylated/unmethylated counts per site) must be collapsed into genomic regions before differential methylation testing. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/genomic-region-coordinate-aggregation))
- **genomic-region-coordinate-querying**: Use when the user names a specific chromosomal interval (e.g. 12:11396601-11436500) and asks for its copy number genotype or region-level RD/BAF statistics from an existing .pytor archive. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/genomic-region-coordinate-querying))
- **genomic-region-masking-with-bedtools**: Use when building a reference for tncRNA (tRF/tRNA half) detection where reads mapping to non-tRNA loci would otherwise be misattributed, and tRNA regions plus 50 nt flanks must be masked before adding artificial tRNA chromosomes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/genomic-region-masking-with-bedtools))
- **genomic-region-overlap-querying**: Use when you have TF binding locations (e.g. an ENCODE ChIP-seq peak BED file) and need to find which EpiRegio REMs overlap them and which target genes those REMs are associated with. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/genomic-region-overlap-querying))
- **genomic-region-promoter-intergenic-intragenic-assignment**: Use when BED peak lists (e.g. MACS-called H3K4me3, H3K27me3, or bivalent peaks) must be classified into promoter, intergenic, or intragenic features using a RefSeq refGene annotation for a genome assembly such as mouse mm9. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/genomic-region-promoter-intergenic-intragenic-assignment))
- **genomic-region-sequence-handling**: Use when you need per-region pairwise comparisons of homologous sequences (e.g., BLAST-derived region bins from metagenomic assemblies) quantified as synteny scores rather than SNP distances. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/genomic-region-sequence-handling))
- **genomic-region-set-enrichment-analysis**: Use when testing whether a genomic feature (R-loops, G-quadruplexes, GC/CpG content) is enriched on HOT region coordinates relative to control regions binned by TF occupancy percentiles. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/genomic-region-set-enrichment-analysis))
- **genomic-region-set-processing**: Use when you hold a set of chromosome coordinates from epigenomic profiling (e.g., ATAC-seq DAR peaks called by MACS2 or DiffBind) and need to identify transcriptional regulators for that region set via BIT. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/genomic-region-set-processing))
- **genomic-region-shuffling-negative-control**: Use when benchmarking TPX-prediction software (3plex, Triplexator, TriplexAligner, fasim-LongTarget) and a matched randomised genomic negative set is needed to compute ROC AUCs against experimentally derived positive peaks. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/genomic-region-shuffling-negative-control))
- **genomic-region-span-verification**: Use when a published pipeline reports a total differential-region span (e.g. Mb or % of genome) and you must reproduce the pipeline and confirm your summed call-set span matches that value. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/genomic-region-span-verification))
- **genomic-region-subsetting-of-hic-data**: Use when Microcket-generated .hic contact-matrix files (e.g., QSC and FISC Hi-C replicates) must be visualized as contact maps for specific regions, resolutions, and downstream comparative analyses. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/genomic-region-subsetting-of-hic-data))
- **genomic-region-to-ctx-region-mapping**: Use when binarized topic or cluster region sets from single-cell ATAC-seq (e.g. cisTopic topics) must be converted to the region identifiers of a cistarget/RcisTarget database (e.g. hg19-regions-9species) before running motif enrichment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/genomic-region-to-ctx-region-mapping))
- **genomic-regions-annotation**: Annotate genomic regions from epigenetic NGS (ATAC-seq, ChIP-seq, CUT&Tag, CUT&RUN, differential peaks) to nearby genes, genomic features, and/or chromatin states (ChromHMM Roadmap, Segway/ENCODE, or custom dense BED models). Use for peak-to-gene annotation, genomic feature assignment, ChromHMM/Segway state annotation, closest chromatin-state model lookup, header-free BED, VOUT, reporting, visualization, and GSEA-ready exports. Requires an explicitly stated genome build. ([stjudecab/CAB-aiSkills](https://github.com/stjudecab/CAB-aiSkills/tree/HEAD/genomic-regions-annotation) · ★6)
- **genomic-regions-correlation**: Compare two genomic-region BED files with GenometriCorr and produce reciprocal correlation reports and visualizations for hg19, hg38, or mm10. Use when asked to quantify genomic-region overlap, distance, distribution, or spatial correlation between two BED region sets. ([stjudecab/CAB-aiSkills](https://github.com/stjudecab/CAB-aiSkills/tree/HEAD/genomic-regions-correlation) · ★6)
- **genomic-resolution-matching-resampling**: Use when benchmarking scRNA-seq inferred copy number profiles against bulk WGS/WES ground truth whose native resolution differs from the single-cell profile bins, requiring harmonization before correlation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/genomic-resolution-matching-resampling))
- **genomic-scaling-benchmark-profiling**: Use when evaluating whether a tiled/smoothed genome-wide model implementation's runtime and memory footprint scale linearly with parameters per tile, or when reproducing version-to-version speedup benchmarks (e.g. 2-3 orders of magnitude). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/genomic-scaling-benchmark-profiling))
- **genomic-sequence-anchor-alignment**: Use when sequences are centered on an anchor point (e.g. TSS) with a continuous per-sequence score, and the goal is to find motif positions whose occurrence correlates with that score. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/genomic-sequence-anchor-alignment))
- **genomic-sequence-bin-feature-extraction**: Use when you need per-position (binned) DNA sequence and/or RNA-Seq coverage features over transcript 3' terminal exons from a GTF to feed a sequence+coverage machine-learning model such as aptardi's biLSTM. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/genomic-sequence-bin-feature-extraction))
- **genomic-sequence-composition-analysis**: Use when designing k-mer-based sequence detectors and you need to choose the k-mer length k: sweep k over a candidate range and compute the analytical false-positive rate of spurious k-mer occurrence for given target/non-target sequence lengths. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/genomic-sequence-composition-analysis))
- **genomic-sequence-data-preparation**: Use when a training run for deepC (or a similar sequence-based chromatin interaction model) needs genome sequence windows plus 5 kb Hi-C skeleton targets assembled from raw genome assemblies, BED intervals, and Hi-C contact data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/genomic-sequence-data-preparation))
- **genomic-sequence-extraction-around-anchor-motifs**: Use when a positional motif enrichment analysis (e.g. MEPP) requires centered sequences around anchors such as deduplicated, RepeatMasker-filtered motif sites, TSSs, or cleavage sites, with a score attached per sequence. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/genomic-sequence-extraction-around-anchor-motifs))
- **genomic-sequence-feature-encoding**: Use when raw enhancer FASTA sequences (e.g. data/train/NHLF.fasta) must be converted into fixed-dimensional numeric vectors before training or predicting with a deep learning model such as Enhancer-MDLF. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/genomic-sequence-feature-encoding))
- **genomic-sequence-motif-scanning**: Use when you have scored sequences (e.g. ATAC-seq log2 fold-change scores on ±100 bp windows around a GATA1 motif) in FASTA with score headers and need to locate occurrences of known motifs from a fixed JASPAR/HOMER library for positional enrichment analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/genomic-sequence-motif-scanning))
- **genomic-sequence-preprocessing**: Use when computing background-adjusted alignment-free measures (CVtree, D2star, D2shepp) on genomic FASTA files or NGS shotgun reads and the Markov model order encoding the sequence is unknown to the user. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/genomic-sequence-preprocessing))
- **genomic-sequence-retrieval-ncbi**: Use when a downstream analysis (e.g. LCS cross-contamination ROC curves or Bowtie2 alignment for read recruitment) requires the exact NCBI RefSeq viral genomes cited by accession, such as NC_003977.2 or NC_004102.1. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/genomic-sequence-retrieval-ncbi))
- **genomic-set-analysis**: Order-independent overlap and combinatorial analysis of genomic region sets (ChIP-seq, ATAC-seq, CUT&Tag, CUT&RUN, narrowPeak/broadPeak/BED) or gene sets (GMT) using Intervene (Venn / UpSet / pairwise) plus optional pairwise Fisher exact overlap significance (default on). Builds a union of elements, a membership matrix, and mutually exclusive per-sector files, then optionally chains the genomic-regions-annotation skill for nearby-gene annotation and the pathway-enrichment-enrichr skill for Enrichr pathway enrichment of both the intersection sectors and the original inputs, plus gated expressi… ([stjudecab/CAB-aiSkills](https://github.com/stjudecab/CAB-aiSkills/tree/HEAD/genomic-set-analysis) · ★6)
- **genomic-source-metadata-tabulation**: Use when you have PoDP project JSON records containing (meta)genome entries with GenBank accession numbers or JGI Genome IDs and need organism names attached per project/genome label for downstream enrichment or filtering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/genomic-source-metadata-tabulation))
- **genomic-subgroup-classification**: Use when given a high-dimensional gene expression or methylation matrix (rows = features, columns = samples) and the goal is to assign samples to molecular subgroups without known labels. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/genomic-subgroup-classification))
- **genomic-taxonomy-aggregation**: Use when you have a BiG-SLiCE output SQLite database plus genome taxonomy annotations (e.g., GTDB 04-RS89) and need per-taxon BGC class proportions or GCF diversity summaries. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/genomic-taxonomy-aggregation))
- **genomic-tile-standard-error-computation**: Use when you need pointwise standard errors or variance of GAM smooth estimates for base-level significance testing in GenoGAM tile fits, where the penalized Hessian is large and sparse so a full inverse is infeasible. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/genomic-tile-standard-error-computation))
- **genomic-tiled-model-fitting**: Use when base-level ChIP-Seq count data along large chromosomes must be modeled with smooth functions genome-wide, and a full-genome GAM fit is intractable without tiling and sparse-matrix parameter fitting. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/genomic-tiled-model-fitting))
- **genomic-to-protein-position-variant-mapping**: Use when SNVs from gnomAD r2.0.2 or ClinVar VCFs must be projected onto UniProt/Swiss-Prot residues and Pfam consensus positions for gene-level or meta-domain tolerance analyses. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/genomic-to-protein-position-variant-mapping))
- **genomic-track-alignment-and-integration**: Use when one or few DNase-seq (or ChIP-seq) replicates are available and a BIRD-predicted DH track can be merged with observed signal on shared genomic bins, then benchmarked against a held-out truth replicate via genome-wide Pearson correlation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/genomic-track-alignment-and-integration))
- **genomic-track-file-compression**: Use when filtered .bam/.bai alignments from a ChIPseq or RNAseq pipeline must be converted into genome-browser track files (wig/bigwig) or compact GRanges objects for visualization and downstream analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/genomic-track-file-compression))
- **genomic-track-prediction-across-species**: Use when reproducing or extending the RSAP PCC benchmark table in DNALONGBENCH, i.e., when fine-tuned DNA foundation models must be evaluated against expert and CNN baselines on human and mouse regulatory activity tracks. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/genomic-track-prediction-across-species))
- **genomic-variant-annotation-review**: Use when a task involves reviewing a genomic variant-annotation workflow for reference build, transcript, evidence, and reproducibility to identify the research or operational question, target population or system, relevant version, sensitive data, and approval boundary before acting. Use current primary sources, produce a traceable artifact, and verify it against explicit criteria. Trigger for planning, analysis, review, or troubleshooting in this focused domain; do not execute external writes or clinical actions without authorization. ([Manoj-11-Dahal/try-Skills](https://github.com/Manoj-11-Dahal/try-Skills/tree/HEAD/skills/science-research/life-sciences/genomic-variant-annotation-review) · ★3)
- **genomic-variant-dataset-curation**: Use when building a supervised deep learning training set of non-coding variants requiring curated positive (dsQTL, eQTL, HGMD) and frequency-matched negative SNPs, as for ncDeepBrain. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/genomic-variant-dataset-curation))
- **genomic-variant-identifier-mapping**: Use when variant lists (single variants or CSV/VCF batches) must be converted into REST API queries against annotation services like MyVariant.info, or when extending the pipeline with a new API-based annotation source such as DisGeNET. - **genomic-variant-qc-validation**: Use when a raw ClinVar VCF must be cleaned, normalized, and QC-validated to a fixed variant count and clinical-significance composition before it is used as a benchmark for annotation-tool concordance (ANNOVAR, SnpEff, VEP). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/genomic-variant-qc-validation))
- **genomic-variant-tensor-encoding**: Use when calling SNVs/small indels from Illumina, PacBio or Oxford Nanopore (SMS) BAMs with a deep learning caller, and you must convert pileups at candidate loci into fixed-size numeric tensors for a CNN. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/genomic-variant-tensor-encoding))
- **genomic-variants**: Read before you filter, annotate, or test called variants or genotype cohorts (GWAS, Mendelian randomization). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/genomic-vs-metagenomic-assembler-group-comparison))
- **genomic-window-tiling**: Use when generating fixed candidate binding-site windows over GENCODE/Ensembl GFF annotations for CLIP-seq analysis (Skipper-style), replacing peak calling with annotation-aware tiling. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/genomic-window-tiling))

([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/genotype-maf-filtering))
- **genotype-phenotype-association-interpretation**: Use when a trained Traitar phenotype model (phypat or phypat+PGL) yields a ranked list of phenotype-relevant Pfam families and you need to assign biological meaning, accessions, and phenotype-to-family mappings for a trait of interest. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/genotype-phenotype-association-interpretation))
- **genotype-phenotype-concordance-analysis**: Use when you have per-sample predicted resistance calls in JSON from Mykrobe predictor plus phenotypic DST labels and need sensitivity/specificity per antibiotic, or when reproducing error tables like St_B1's 99.1%/99.6% or minor-allele sensitivity deltas. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/genotype-phenotype-concordance-analysis))
- **genotype-phenotype-data-preparation**: Use when when you have raw genotype and phenotype data files (e.g., from a GWAS dataset or genetic study) and need to apply PaIRKAT or similar pathway-level association tests. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/genotype-phenotype-data-preparation))
- **genotype-phenotype-inference-assessment**: Use when you need to assess the accuracy of phenotype prediction from bacterial genomes, e.g., computing per-phylum accuracies of phypat vs. phypat+PGL on pooled GIDEON cross-validation predictions for phyla covered by at least five species. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/genotype-phenotype-inference-assessment))
- **genotype-quality-filtering**: Use when InDel genotypes have been called for 2 parents and progeny (e.g., an F1 Populus hybrid population with 20 progeny) and non-Mendelian or low-quality marker sites must be removed before linkage mapping.
